The teacher-prescribed week and readable 2D evidence ship first.\\r\\n- **Response popularity and score predictions:** a sparse or unvalidated comparison group would imply false precision.\\r\\n- **Old browser-only exam import:** owner cannot be proven. Preserve copies untouched and outside account Attempts.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe full audit can inform later refinements. It does not imply that competitor features or these tickets are already implemented in LSAT U.\\r\\n\\r\\n## First verified slice (2026-09-28)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe first code slice adds the seven-destination rail and locked URL tabs, a scoped My Work ledger with student notes and Joshua\'s recaps, a saved LR wrong/flagged Review queue, a distinct first-answer Progress card in student and selected-student views, and an actor-owned Home next action. It preserves separate assignment instances and does not mark practice complete merely because a page was opened. The timed LR section now waits for an account save before question navigation. Local typecheck, production build, 157 focused tests, and signed-in browser checks passed. Home, My Work, Review, and Progress were checked at 390, 820, 1060, 1280, and 1600 CSS pixels for page overflow; the Progress detail table scrolls internally on narrow screens.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis is partial coverage. The Review queue currently draws from saved LR wrong-answer and flag records; RC, Activity, bootcamp, and teacher-selected review tasks are still open. Full PrepTest report expansion and RC save recovery are coded locally but held out of the first commit pending a completed-test browser fixture and backend failure verification. Live student-role and RLS checks are still needed. Exact drill selection, full History and topic evidence, Library topic hub, Schedule chain, selected-student controls, study tools, settings, and the final Impeccable pass remain open. No ticket should be closed solely because this slice ships.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Second local slice (2026-09-28)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe next code slice adds completed standalone RC wrong answers and flags to Review, with exact question links; a saved-answer History page and LR Topics page with a persisted 7/14/21/30/all-day window; and clearer exact-drill pool counts with an all-question set preview. RC activity, bootcamp, active-session, and practice-test questions remain outside the Review queue until they have reliable exact-item review routes. Topics use saved LR question types only; sections remain all time because their source dates are not reliable enough to filter. The drill builder still needs saved run lifecycle, Inspect mode, and feedback-mode behavior before ticket #171 is complete.\\r\\n\\r\\nIntegrated typecheck, production build, and 54 consolidated tests in eight relevant files passed. Local browser reads of Review, History, and Topics passed at 390px, 900px, and 1440px without page overflow. The drill builder showed the actual available count and opened a ten-question exact-set preview at each of those widths. A scoped code review reported no high-confidence material defect. The second slice was manually published through Lovable. A signed-in student account showed the saved-answer History filters and LR Topics evidence on `lsatprep.study`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Third local slice (2026-09-28)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe next slice adds a written Main Conclusion lesson pilot to Library, drawing its rule and worked example from existing Joshua-authored material. It links to a filtered drill and shows saved first-answer evidence when the account permits it. Joshua\'s selected-student dossier gains Review and Progress tabs with explicit actor/subject context. Review lists saved first-answer misses for the selected student; it does not create a scored teacher attempt. These pages are bounded pilots, not completion of #175 or #177. The Library still needs more topic hubs and Activity/bootcamp improvements. Teacher Review still needs flags, wrong-answer notes, exact question inspection, and a fuller control walkthrough.\\r\\n\\r\\nLocal browser checks loaded the lesson and both teacher tabs at desktop and 390px without page overflow. Four focused tests, integrated TypeScript checking, and the production build passed. A scoped code review found no material issue at confidence of 80% or greater. An Impeccable technical pass on the new views found and corrected a nested lesson panel, a heavy quote border, low-contrast teacher helper text, an untokenized background, and undersized teacher links. The third slice reached `main` as `5b5db77` after merging concurrent question-bank corrections. Lovable published it, and a signed-in student account showed the new Main Conclusion lesson at `lsatprep.study/library/lessons`. Live teacher-account verification remains open; teacher Review and Progress were checked in a signed-in local browser.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe full PrepTest result detail and RC save-recovery changes remain local and unpublished. Completed-run browser evidence and backend failure behavior are required before those paths can be released.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Fourth local slice (2026-09-28)\\r\\n\\r\\nSaved Sets gains a read-only Inspect action. It shows the exact stored questions in order, with stimulus, stem, and answer choices, without launching a drill or writing exposures or attempts. A missing bank question is labeled unavailable instead of silently replaced. A signed-in local browser showed an eight-question set, expanded the first question, and rendered the dialog at 390px without page overflow. The new Inspect, close, and question-expansion targets measure at least 44px high on that viewport. Two focused no-write and owner-guard tests, integrated TypeScript checking, and the production build passed; a scoped review found no material issue. The fourth slice was pushed to `main` as `09ff7e2` and Lovable confirmed its publication. This covers Inspect for saved sets only. Durable per-run Continue, Results, and Review remain open because the current session key can be reused by a later run of the same pool.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Fifth local slice (2026-09-28)\\r\\n\\r\\nThe held full PrepTest question report and RC save recovery are now integrated locally. A signed-in teacher-owned PrepTest 159 run completed all three legs in the local app: LR, RC, LR. The report showed all three section details, saved first answers and timing, inline read-only LR/RC question text for students without Drill access, and exact links for accounts with Drill access. It had no page overflow at 390, 820, 1060, 1280, and 1600 CSS pixels. In a timed RC drill, simulated network loss paused the clock and blocked question navigation; restoring the connection and retrying allowed navigation. Save and exit waited for account writes, then a reopened drill restored the saved answers and place. Reloading a completed RC leg returned to the test report. A second teacher-owned PrepTest 158 run verified that the RC leg opens its own authorized session directly and resumes the same session after exit. RC answer retries reuse a client attempt ID after a lost response, preventing duplicate rows. Under a delayed account save, answer choices were visibly disabled until navigation finished, and returning to the question restored the saved choice. Assigned-only authorization has focused route tests, but no live assigned-only account was available for a browser check. The final 29 focused tests, typecheck, production build, and diff check passed. Commit `9dc3627` reached `main`, Lovable published it, and a fresh live bundle contained the RC save gate. The full audit remains incomplete.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Sixth local slice (2026-09-28)\\r\\n\\r\\nStandalone custom LR drills now save each sitting under its own account-backed run ID. The Drills area shows Create, In progress, Completed, and Saved sets tabs. In progress appears after an answer is saved; Continue reopens the exact queue and answers. Completed retains repeated sittings of the same set and opens read-only Results and question-by-question Review. Saved sets currently opens the existing set-management page from its tab. Smart Drill, RC, guided drills, and assigned work keep their separate histories and are explicitly outside this run list for now.\\r\\n\\r\\nIn a signed-in local browser, one two-question run saved after the first answer, resumed on question two in another tab, and completed with a persisted result and written answer-choice review. A repeated run of the same two questions appeared as a second completed record; both remained visible. A focused save-failure test confirmed that Next holds the chosen question when run creation fails. The runner\'s numbered-question jumps were brought through the same account-save gate. Five focused files passed 92 tests, typecheck and production build passed, and the Drills tabs had no page overflow at 390, 820, 1060, 1280, or 1600 CSS pixels. A 390px completed Review also had no overflow. Commit `2562479` reached `main`, Lovable reported \\"Your website was updated,\\" and a fresh signed-in live page showed the tabs, both completed test runs, and Results. The full audit remains incomplete.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Seventh slice: completed Activity misses in Review\\r\\n\\r\\nThe Review queue now adds each judged missed item from a completed Activity assignment. It keeps repeated assignments separate and links to the exact item, its saved attempt sequence, credited answer, and authored explanation. In-progress assignments and unjudged spoken-recall or section work stay out of this queue. This slice also excludes guided sets from the standalone custom LR run ledger. A signed-in local browser loaded the revised queue and the unavailable-state Activity detail route; this account had no completed Activity miss to inspect end to end, so the exact-item rendering was checked with a focused owned-assignment test. Bootcamp, skipped-question, active RC, practice-test, and teacher-selected review sources remain open.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Eighth slice: opened and blank timed LR questions\\r\\n\\r\\nCompleted standalone LR section history now contributes questions the student opened and left blank to a separate Left blank Review filter. The record does not prove a deliberate skip, so the UI avoids that claim. Each section sitting has its own queue item and owned read-only detail route with the saved visit and answer state plus current bank question wording. Completed PrepTest legs and legacy rows without visit data stay out of this source. Some of these questions also exist in the wrong-answer journal; the section entry retains the sitting context rather than replacing the journal entry. A signed-in local browser showed the Left blank filter and the inaccessible-item route. This teacher account had no qualifying sitting to open live; focused tests cover the exact saved-item view. Bootcamp, active RC, full PrepTest, and teacher-selected review remain open.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Ninth slice: completed PrepTest items in Review\\r\\n\\r\\nCompleted account-owned PrepTests now contribute saved first-answer misses and flags from LR and RC, plus LR questions opened and left blank when visit evidence exists. Incomplete legs, unvisited blanks, RC questions without a first attempt, legacy PT118 structures, RC sessions whose passages do not match the test leg, and older records without question detail remain outside this source. Queue identity includes the sitting, leg, and question; duplicate journal items remain labeled by source. Each item links to the exact question inside the completed score report and returns to the Review filter. A read-only account run loader prevents opening Review from importing device backups. A fresh signed-in live browser showed separate journal and completed PrepTest items for PT159, then followed Section 1 Question 1 to its saved A first answer and E correct answer in the score report. Commit `5f6e89f` reached `main` and Lovable\'s Publish control completed.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Tenth slice: flagged questions in active RC drills\\r\\n\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"6d86d1","wall_time_seconds":1.7741144,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":2106,"output":"| **6. Connect learning and sessions** | Finish [#175](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/175) with two text-first Activities, one bootcamp, assigned exact exercise links, resume/finish, and authored topic pages. Then finish [#176](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/176) with one session\'s prep, recap, and follow-up through Schedule, My Work, and teacher dossier. | Saved work resumes exactly; completion is source-backed; authored explanations remain Joshua\'s; session links refer to the same event with correct timezone and honest absent states. Potential credit-spending actions still require immediate confirmation.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Release method for every remaining step\\r\\n\\r\\nFoundation follow-up identified on 2026-09-30 and resolved by the twenty-fourth slice: `ActivityRunner.tsx` previously routed historical `status = completed` rows to an empty report without a completion timestamp or saved responses. Proposed server work is documented in `docs/implementation-plans/2026-09-30-timed-write-contract.md` and `2026-09-30-timed-write-implementation.md` in the app repo.\\r\\n\\r\\n1. Update the coverage matrix and this plan; close only the acceptance checks actually proved.\\r\\n\\r\\n## What remains deliberately open\\r\\n\\r\\n### Next-pass scope agreed on 2026-09-30\\r\\n\\r\\nFourteen functional tickets (#166 through #180, excluding deferred #178) remain open; this counts acceptance scope, not fourteen untouched features. The RC builder now holds the inspected passage IDs through launch and shows passage and question text without the answer key. |\\n| First-answer diagnostic Progress with sample size and missing coverage | Include | [#166](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/166), [#173](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/173) | Existing Analytics and History calculations differ; trace every numerator and denominator. |\\n| Summary above detailed, filterable History and topic evidence leading to Review or Practice | Include | [#173](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/173) | First-answer Progress stays visible while the independent legacy rollup loads or fails. Saved-answer History groups filtered rows by date; LR Topics and 7/14/21/30/All remain. Full-session dates and missing source coverage remain open. |\\n| Small written reasoning exercises, clear instructions, progress, and finish state | Include | [#175](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/175) | Improve existing Activities and bootcamps; no new video dependency. |\\n| Topic hub centered on Joshua\'s written rule and worked example | Include | [#175](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/175) | Main Conclusion written pilot, direct drill, and account-specific evidence are live; further authored topics remain open. |\\n| One-to-one session prep, recap, and follow-up chain | Include | [#176](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/176) | Reuse Schedule and typed recap records; preserve timezone and booking clarity. |\\n| Teacher authoring and selected-student view with full controls | Include | [#177](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/177) | Selected-student Review and Progress are published and locally teacher-verified; live teacher check, notes/flags, exact inspection, and full controls remain open. |\\n| Separate bookmarks, passage find/highlight/elimination, optional second attributed explanation | Include after core | [#179](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/179) | Only use tools the question surface supports; do not conflate bookmark and flag. |\\n| Consequence-aware accommodations, feedback, review, analytics, contrast, text-size settings | Include after core | [#180](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/180) | Persist provenance; no retroactive score changes. |\\n| Calm card hierarchy, one primary action, restrained color, readable explanation pane | Include as cross-cutting design | [#168](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/168), [#170](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/170), [#178](https://github.com/vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db/issues/178) | Apply LSAT U tokens, inspect phone/laptop/wide/sidecar, then run Impeccable critique and fixes. |\\n\\n## Ideas we are not building in this release\\n\\n- **New video catalog, group classes, broad forum, tutor marketplace, leaderboards, generic streak pressure:** poor fit for the current one-to-one offer or require ongoing operations Joshua has not chosen.\\n- **LawHub or paper-score import:** ownership, rights, consent, source mapping, and actual demand need a separate decision. Existing results remain available.\\n- **Automatic capacity-based scheduling and a 3D analytics hero:** these are separate product and performance bets. The teacher-prescribed week and readable 2D evidence ship first.\\n- **Response popularity and score predictions:** a sparse or unvalidated comparison group would imply false precision.\\n- **Old browser-only exam import:** owner cannot be proven. Preserve copies untouched and outside account Attempts.\\n\\nThe full audit can inform later refinements. It does not imply that competitor features or these tickets are already implemented in LSAT U.\\n\\n## First verified slice (2026-09-28)\\n\\nThe first code slice adds the seven-destination rail and locked URL tabs, a scoped My Work ledger with student notes and Joshua\'s recaps, a saved LR wrong/flagged Review queue, a distinct first-answer Progress card in student and selected-student views, and an actor-owned Home next action. It preserves separate assignment instances and does not mark practice complete merely because a page was opened. The timed LR section now waits for an account save before question navigation. Local typecheck, production build, 157 focused tests, and signed-in browser checks passed. Home, My Work, Review, and Progress were checked at 390, 820, 1060, 1280, and 1600 CSS pixels for page overflow; the Progress detail table scrolls internally on narrow screens.\\n\\nThis is partial coverage. The Review queue currently draws from saved LR wrong-answer and flag records; RC, Activity, bootcamp, and teacher-selected review tasks are still open. Full PrepTest report expansion and RC save recovery are coded locally but held out of the first commit pending a completed-test browser fixture and backend failure verification. Live student-role and RLS checks are still needed. Exact drill selection, full History and topic evidence, Library topic hub, Schedule chain, selected-student controls, study tools, settings, and the final Impeccable pass remain open. No ticket should be closed solely because this slice ships.\\n\\n## Second local slice (2026-09-28)\\n\\nThe next code slice adds completed standalone RC wrong answers and flags to Review, with exact question links; a saved-answer History page and LR Topics page with a persisted 7/14/21/30/all-day window; and clearer exact-drill pool counts with an all-question set preview. RC activity, bootcamp, active-session, and practice-test questions remain outside the Review queue until they have reliable exact-item review routes. Topics use saved LR question types only; sections remain all time because their source dates are not reliable enough to filter. The drill builder still needs saved run lifecycle, Inspect mode, and feedback-mode behavior before ticket #171 is complete.\\n\\nIntegrated typecheck, production build, and 54 consolidated tests in eight relevant files passed. Local browser reads of Review, History, and Topics passed at 390px, 900px, and 1440px without page overflow. The drill builder showed the actual available count and opened a ten-question exact-set preview at each of those widths. A scoped code review reported no high-confidence material defect. The second slice was manually published through Lovable. A signed-in student account showed the saved-answer History filters and LR Topics evidence on `lsatprep.study`.\\n\\n## Third local slice (2026-09-28)\\n\\nThe next slice adds a written Main Conclusion lesson pilot to Library, drawing its rule and worked example from existing Joshua-authored material. It links to a filtered drill and shows saved first-answer evidence when the account permits it. Joshua\'s selected-student dossier gains Review and Progress tabs with explicit actor/subject context. Review lists saved first-answer misses for the selected student; it does not create a scored teacher attempt. These pages are bounded pilots, not completion of #175 or #177. The Library still needs more topic hubs and Activity/bootcamp improvements. Teacher Review still needs flags, wrong-answer notes, exact question inspection, and a fuller control walkthrough.\\n\\nLocal browser checks loaded the lesson and both teacher tabs at desktop and 390px without page overflow. Four focused tests, integrated TypeScript checking, and the production build passed. A scoped code review found no material issue at confidence of 80% or greater. An Impeccable technical pass on the new views found and corrected a nested lesson panel, a heavy quote border, low-contrast teacher helper text, an untokenized background, and undersized teacher links. The third slice reached `main` as `5b5db77` after merging concurrent question-bank corrections. Lovable published it, and a signed-in student account showed the new Main Conclusion lesson at `lsatprep.study/library/lessons`. Live teacher-account verification remains open; teacher Review and Progress were checked in a signed-in local browser.\\n\\nThe full PrepTest result detail and RC save-recovery changes remain local and unpublished. Completed-run browser evidence and backend failure behavior are required before those paths can be released.\\n\\n## Fourth local slice (2026-09-28)\\n\\nSaved Sets gains a read-only Inspect action. It shows the exact stored questions in order, with stimulus, stem, and answer choices, without launching a drill or writing exposures or attempts. A missing bank question is labeled unavailable instead of silently replaced. A signed-in local browser showed an eight-question set, expanded the first question, and rendered the dialog at 390px without page overflow. The new Inspect, close, and question-expansion targets measure at least 44px high on that viewport. Two focused no-write and owner-guard tests, integrated TypeScript checking, and the production build passed; a scoped review found no material issue. The fourth slice was pushed to `main` as `09ff7e2` and Lovable confirmed its publication. This covers Inspect for saved sets only. Durable per-run Continue, Results, and Review remain open because the current session key can be reused by a later run of the same pool.\\n\\n## Fifth local slice (2026-09-28)\\n\\nThe held full PrepTest question report and RC save recovery are now integrated locally. A signed-in teacher-owned PrepTest 159 run completed all three legs in the local app: LR, RC, LR. The report showed all three section details, saved first answers and timing, inline read-only LR/RC question text for students without Drill access, and exact links for accounts with Drill access. It had no page overflow at 390, 820, 1060, 1280, and 1600 CSS pixels. In a timed RC drill, simulated network loss paused the clock and blocked question navigation; restoring the connection and retrying allowed navigation. Save and exit waited for account writes, then a reopened drill restored the saved answers and place. Reloading a completed RC leg returned to the test report. A second teacher-owned PrepTest 158 run verified that the RC leg opens its own authorized session directly and resumes the same session after exit. RC answer retries reuse a client attempt ID after a lost response, preventing duplicate rows. Under a delayed account save, answer choices were visibly disabled until navigation finished, and returning to the question restored the saved choice. Assigned-only authorization has focused route tests, but no live assigned-only account was available for a browser check. The final 29 focused tests, typecheck, production build, and diff check passed. Commit `9dc3627` reached `main`, Lovable published it, and a fresh live bundle contained the RC save gate. The full audit remains incomplete.\\n\\n## Sixth local slice (2026-09-28)\\n\\nStandalone custom LR drills now save each sitting under its own account-backed run ID. The Drills area shows Create, In progress, Completed, and Saved sets tabs. In progress appears after an answer is saved; Continue reopens the exact…1602 tokens truncated… completion from its source task. Guided assignments mark their owned assignment and linked task complete when practice finishes, while a saved Smart Drill appears in In progress only after an answer. Progress first-answer evidence loads independently of the older breakdown, and History groups saved answers by date.\\n\\nSmart Drill now has a durable account-owned run ID for each sitting. Its completed Results and Review use only questions attempted in that sitting; an old shared resume row can still be resumed and is closed when the migrated sitting finishes. Both Next controls wait for an account save. Focused Smart Drill tests passed 10/10, typecheck and production build passed, and commit `55f3aec` reached GitHub `main` on 2026-09-29. Lovable missed the first push event. An empty `a6deac2` commit, sent through the guarded push script, triggered import. Lovable reported \\"Your website was updated.\\" A fresh signed-in live Drills page showed two distinct Smart Drill sittings; the newer Results and Review each contained its one answered question. These local browser checks wrote two completed sittings under Joshua\'s teacher account.\\n\\nThis is still partial audit coverage. Teacher-selected review actions, broader Library and Schedule integration, study tools, settings, and the remaining ticket details still need verification and implementation. Joshua deferred the final Impeccable pass while functional slices continue.\\n\\n## Seventeenth slice: distinct guided drill sittings\\n\\nGuided assignments and directly launched guided drills now save each sitting under an account-owned run ID. A student can resume the same frozen question queue, review earlier completed sittings, and start another without replacing prior Results. The old singleton session remains available as a legacy result until its equivalent ledger run exists. Answer navigation waits for an account save; a missing saved question stops resume instead of replacing the queue. Finishing freezes the saved answer set before analytics writes, then uses one stable attempt ID per run and question, so a failed Finish retry or reload cannot double-count or change the result. Early-End Results and Review count attempted questions only. Teacher practice stays owned by the teacher account.\\n\\nLocal signed-in checks used the \\"Reconcile the Pivot\\" guided drill: three separate sittings were saved under Joshua\'s teacher account; an unfinished sitting resumed its exact next question with the same run ID; a one-answer early finish displayed `0 / 1` and one Review question. Focused tests passed 35/35 after the final closeout fix, typecheck and production build passed, and a scoped re-review found no material regression. Commit `a33fcac` reached GitHub `main` on 2026-09-29. The first Lovable publish attempt failed while the new revision was still previewing; a retry updated the site. A fresh signed-in live guided drill page listed all three completed sittings, and the early-finish Results showed `0 / 1` from one answered question. The local browser checks wrote three completed sittings under Joshua\'s teacher account.\\n\\n## Eighteenth slice: saved Intro to LR interactive misses in Review\\n\\nReview now reads missed `intro_lr_attempts` under the signed-in account and gives each saved attempt its own queue item and read-only detail route. The detail uses the stored answer and current authored question and reasoning; opening it does not launch a runner or write a new attempt. Authored IDs from the earlier 3.1 through 3.5, 3.7, and 3.9 interactive exercises are supported. Unknown or retired IDs are withheld rather than linked to the wrong content. The base table retains attempt IDs and repeat sittings, unlike the older union history view. Owner and missed-answer filters apply to both the list and exact-detail fetch.\\n\\nThis restores access to historical interactive exercise misses. Current native Intro to LR labs and Hard Sentences do not use this authored-ID adapter and are not claimed as covered. Do not hand-edit generated types.\\r\\n- `supabase/functions/`: Edge Functions.\\r\\n- `supabase/migrations/`: database schema migrations.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Stack And Commands\\r\\n\\r\\n- Stack: React 18, Vite 5, TypeScript, Tailwind, shadcn/ui, Supabase, TanStack Query.\\r\\n- Package manager: `npm`. `package-lock.json` is canonical. Ignore stray Bun lockfiles.\\r\\n- Dev server: `npm run dev` on port `8080`.\\r\\n- Build: `npm run build`\\r\\n- Lint: `npm run lint`\\r\\n- Test: `npm run test`\\r\\n- Watch tests: `npm run test:watch`\\r\\n- Typecheck: `npm run typecheck`. Required before a push — `vite build` strips types without checking them, so a type error would ship. `.claude/safe-push.sh` gates on it.\\r\\n- E2E: `npm run test:e2e` (Playwright, `e2e/smoke.spec.ts`).\\r\\n\\r\\n## Working Rules\\r\\n\\r\\n- Keep changes small and aligned with existing patterns.\\r\\n- Use TypeScript React function components and the `@/` path alias.\\r\\n- Preserve Lovable-specific plumbing, including the `lovable-tagger` Vite plugin, the PWA setup, and the service-worker guard in `src/main.tsx`.\\r\\n- Do not change app behavior just to satisfy tooling setup.\\r\\n- Do not hardcode secrets or service-role credentials.\\r\\n- Treat Supabase-generated files as generated artifacts, not hand-edited source.\\r\\n- TypeScript strictness is intentionally loose. Do not tighten it without explicit approval.\\r\\n- Keep this adapter aligned with `CLAUDE.md` without duplicating the full project guide. Put durable detail in `CLAUDE.md`; keep shared reference material in `docs/`, `references/`, or `templates/`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Product Constraints\\r\\n\\r\\n- The Foyer is the primary navigation surface. The `GooeyDock` quick-launch row belongs only on `/foyer`.\\r\\n- Inbox is a real 1:1 student-admin messaging system with PDF attachments.\\r\\n- Admin surfaces must stay responsive on desktop and mobile.\\r\\n- Prefer truthful fallbacks over fake buttons, fake analytics, or placeholder behavior.\\r\\n- Onboarding asks for name, not username. Sign-in uses email. The instructor display name is `Joshua`.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Smart Drill Note\\r\\n\\r\\nOn `/drill`, adaptive Smart Drill can show a `currentQuestion` that is not the same as `session.questionQueue[session.currentIndex]`. Any per-question UI state must key off `currentQuestion.qid` unless the code path is explicitly limited to queued drills.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Verification (must not skip)\\r\\n\\r\\n- Any change to `src/components/` or `src/pages/` is not \\"done\\" until it has been viewed running in the app (dev server + a browser tool), with a screenshot or DOM check as evidence. Never report a UI change complete on the strength of the diff alone. This is the single biggest recurring failure across past sessions — a hard gate, not a suggestion.\\r\\n- **Responsive fixes carry a wider gate: verify across the full emulate matrix BEFORE pushing, not after Joshua reports it.** Narrow, mid, and wide viewports plus the sidecar-docked case, every time. A pushed \\"fix\\" that does not hold costs a second full session; this has already happened once.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Workflow Expectations\\r\\n\\r\\n- Run `npm run build` before wrapping up significant code or documentation work.\\r\\n- If tests are relevant to the change, run them too.\\r\\n- After completing a change, stage the intended files, commit with a concise message, and push to `origin main` unless the user says otherwise.\\r\\n- **Push via `.claude/safe-push.sh`, not raw `git push`.** Lovable auto-commits to `main` mid-session, so a raw push can be rejected or race a concurrent commit. The script fetches, merges `origin/main` if it diverged (never rebases, never force-pushes), then pushes. It also gates on `npm run typecheck`.\\r\\n- **Never roll `main` back.** Do not use Lovable\'s \\"Revert to commit\\", \\"revert and resend\\", or history restore, and never reset or force-push `main`. On 2026-09-27 an accidental \\"revert and resend\\" (b998596) silently undid three merged PRs across 121 question-bank files, and students saw the wrong questions again. To undo your own work, `git revert <your SHA>` only. If a Lovable dialog offers a revert, dismiss it; if one fires, stop and tell Joshua before repairing anything.\\r\\n- Existing Codex repo hooks live in `.codex/hooks.json`; preserve them unless the task explicitly requires a hook change.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Session Protocol (second brain)\\r\\n\\r\\nProduct context, status, and decisions live in the Obsidian vault at `~/ai-os/`, not this repo. Two repo-local skills in `.agents/skills/` run the session ritual; the canonical protocol is in the vault `CLAUDE.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\nThe vault was rebuilt 2026-07-16 (paths relative to `~/ai-os`). Live: teaching voice `wiki/teaching-voice.md` (route ALL student-facing copy through it), the business `wiki/entities/aspiring-attorneys.md`, a student\'s record `students/<name>/` (see `students/README.md`), teaching knowledge `corpus/index.md`, and the `wiki/{tasks,hot,dashboard,index,log}.md` hubs named below. Archived read-only reference (pre-rebuild product knowledge, may be stale): `archive/wiki-pre-rebuild-2026-07-16/domains/lsat-u/` — product context `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-product.md`, design system `…/synthesis/intro-to-lr-design.md`, quality bar `…/synthesis/bootcamp-quality-bar.md`, domain index `…/index.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n- `pickup` — start of session. Read `wiki/tasks.md` (the Now / Next / Done ledger) first, then `wiki/hot.md`, `wiki/dashboard.md`, skim `wiki/index.md`, read the latest `wiki/log.md` entry. Emit a 6-8 line briefing (where we left off, Now, Next, open threads, hot, suggested next) and wait. Do not deep-read other pages until pointed at a task.\\r\\n- `wrapup` — end of session. Propose which `## Now` tasks finished and confirm before writing; a wrong \\"Done\\" is the failure to avoid. Then strike finished items through in `wiki/tasks.md` with a date, refresh `wiki/hot.md`, append a dated `wiki/log.md` entry (newest first, distinct label for same-day sessions), update the task anchor.\\r\\n\\r\\n`wiki/tasks.md` is the single source of truth for what is open vs done. Finished work is struck through so a new session never re-runs it. `raw/` in the vault is immutable.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Codex Adapter Layout\\r\\n\\r\\n- `.codex/config.toml`: minimal shared Codex project config. Keep secrets and machine-specific overrides out of this file.\\r\\n- `.codex/agents/`: Codex agent definitions converted from `.claude/agents/*.md` when Claude agents exist. Use `.toml` files with converted instructions in `developer_instructions`.\\r\\n- `.agents/skills/`: repository-local Codex skills copied from `.claude/skills/` when Claude skills exist and Codex should use them. Keep each `SKILL.md` with its supporting files.\\r\\n- `.codex/hooks.json`: existing repository hooks. Preserve unless the task explicitly concerns hooks.\\r\\n\\r\\nThis checkout currently has Claude settings and hooks under `.claude/`, but no `.claude/skills/` or `.claude/agents/` directory to convert.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Known Gaps\\r\\n\\r\\n- Vitest specs exist (80 `src/**/*.test.ts(x)` files as of 2026-07-16); run with `npm run test`. Playwright is wired with one e2e spec (`e2e/smoke.spec.ts`), run via `npm run test:e2e`.\\r\\n- No Prettier config and no CI workflows.\\r\\n- `README.md` is still minimal.\\r\\n\\r\\n## Agent skills\\r\\n\\r\\n### Issue tracker\\r\\n\\r\\nGitHub Issues on `vnjkoxpl-42905/lsatu-684d89db`, via the `gh` CLI. See `docs/agents/issue-tracker.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Triage labels\\r\\n\\r\\nThe five canonical roles, unchanged: `needs-triage`, `needs-info`, `ready-for-agent`, `ready-for-human`, `wontfix`. See `docs/agents/triage-labels.md`.\\r\\n\\r\\n### Domain docs\\r\\n\\r\\nMulti-context: `CONTEXT-MAP.md` points at eight per-context glossaries; ADRs are all at the root. See `docs/agents/domain.md`.\\r\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': Nothing here grades, records, flags, or feeds\\r\\n * Review or diagnostics; it is the student\'s own scratch work, and it lives\\r\\n * in this browser\'s local storage only. It does not follow the account to\\r\\n * another device, and the UI must never say it does.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Every record is filed under the signed-in actor\'s id. There is no shared\\r\\n * anonymous bucket: without an actor, nothing is read or written. A teacher\\r\\n * practising a question files aids under the teacher\'s own id, so a selected\\r\\n * student\'s records are never touched.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * A question is named by its family and its canonical id (`lr` + qid such as\\r\\n * `PT156-S2-Q1`, `rc` + the bank\'s numeric question id), so an LR and an RC\\r\\n * question can never share a slot.\\r\\n */\\r\\nimport type { Highlight, HighlightColor } from \'@/lib/highlightUtils\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type StudyFamily = \'lr\' | \'rc\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface StudyQuestionRef {\\r\\n  family: StudyFamily;\\r\\n  /** Canonical id: the LR qid, or the RC question id as a string. */\\r\\n  id: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface StudyBookmark extends StudyQuestionRef {\\r\\n  /** Short human label, e.g. \\"PT156 · S2 · Q1\\". */\\r\\n  label: string;\\r\\n  /** RC only: the passage the question belongs to, for the open link. */\\r\\n  passageId?: number;\\r\\n  addedAt: string;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport interface StudyToolsState {\\r\\n  bookmarks: Record<string, StudyBookmark>;\\r\\n  eliminations: Record<string, string[]>;\\r\\n  highlights: Record<string, Highlight[]>;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport type StudyStoreStatus = \'ok\' | \'unavailable\' | \'recovered\' | \'incompatible\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const STUDY_TOOLS_VERSION = 1;\\r\\nconst KEY_PREFIX = \'lsatu.studyTools.v1:\';\\r\\nconst MAX_BOOKMARKS = 500;\\r\\nconst LETTERS = /^[A-E]$/;\\r\\nconst COLORS: ReadonlySet<HighlightColor> = new Set([\'yellow\', \'pink\', \'orange\', \'underline\']);\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function emptyStudyTools(): StudyToolsState {\\r\\n  return { bookmarks: {}, eliminations: {}, highlights: {} };\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function studyToolsStorageKey(actorId: string): string {\\r\\n  return `${KEY_PREFIX}${actorId}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nexport function studyQuestionKey(ref: StudyQuestionRef): string {\\r\\n  return `${ref.family}:${ref.id}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction validRef(family: unknown, id: unknown): family is StudyFamily {\\r\\n  return (family === \'lr\' || family === \'rc\') && typeof id === \'string\' && id.length > 0 && id.length <= 64;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction keyMatches(key: string, family: StudyFamily, id: string): boolean {\\r\\n  return key === `${family}:${id}`;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction parseKey(key: string): StudyQuestionRef | null {\\r\\n  const i = key.indexOf(\':\');\\r\\n  if (i < 0) return null;\\r\\n  const family = key.slice(0, i);\\r\\n  const id = key.slice(i + 1);\\r\\n  return validRef(family, id) ? { family, id } : React state and URL params cannot do that: both die with the\\r\\n * page. So each practice set keeps one durable row per user in the shared\\r\\n * bootcamp_kv persistence (the same layer module_progress uses), written on\\r\\n * every answer and on every question move.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * These are standalone functions rather than a hook because the drill runner\\r\\n * (src/pages/Drill.tsx) lives outside the bootcamp\'s React tree — the same\\r\\n * reason recordPracticeCompletion.ts exists.\\r\\n *\\r\\n * Failure policy: reads degrade to null (a missing resume row must never block\\r\\n * starting a drill). Writes throw so the caller can surface a toast rather than\\r\\n * silently losing a student\'s place.\\r\\n */\\r\\n\\r\\nimport { getPersistence } from \'@/bootcamps/main-conclusion/data/practiceDrillPools\';\\r\\n\\r\\nexport const PRACTICE_SESSION_DATA_VERSION = 2;\\r\\n\\r\\nconst PT118_S1_ANSWERS: Record<number, string> = {\\r\\n  1: \'A\', 2: \'C\', 3: \'B\', 4: \'C\', 6: \'D\', 7: \'B\', 8: \'A\',\\r\\n  9: \'E\', 10: \'A\', 11: \'C\', 12: \'C\', 13: \'E\', 14: \'E\',\\r\\n  15: \'E\', 16: \'C\', 17: \'B\', 18: \'D\', 19: \'A\', 20: \'D\',\\r\\n  21: \'A\', 22: \'E\', 23: \'C\', 24: \'B\', 25: \'C\', 26: \'E\',\\r\\n};\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction migratePt118Qid(qid: string): string | null {\\r\\n  const match = /^PT118-S([1234])-Q(\\\\d+)$/.exec(qid);\\r\\n  if (!match) return qid;\\r\\n  const section = Number(match[1]);\\r\\n  const number = Number(match[2]);\\r\\n  if (section === 3 && number === 22) return null;\\r\\n  if (section === 1 && number >= 5 && number <= 25) return `PT118-S1-Q${number + 1}`;\\r\\n  return qid;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\nfunction pt118CorrectAnswer(qid: string): string | undefined {\\r\\n  const match = /^PT118-S([12])-Q(\\\\d+)$/.exec(qid);\\r\\n  if (!match) return undefined;\\r\\n  const section = Number(match[1]);\\r\\n  const number = Number(match[2]);\\r\\n  if (section === 1) return PT118_S1_ANSWERS[number];\\r\\n  if (section === 2) return ({ 1: \'D\', 5: \'A\' } as Record<number, string>)[number];\\r\\n  return undefined;\\r\\n}\\r\\n\\r\\n/** Remap ## assistant

The user is right that these areas already contain substantial implementation. They should not be listed as entirely unbuilt.

1. **LR mode/source persistence: genuinely incomplete, but narrow.** Source identity and engine mode already persist in [drillRunLedger.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/lib/drillRunLedger.ts:14). They are `source_key` and mode such as `type-drill`, not the full Training/Test/feedback provenance. Saved snapshot retains queue, answers and timing; `StandaloneDrillRunMeta` contains only mode/label. [DrillRuns.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/pages/DrillRuns.tsx:56) rebuilds Continue through generic question launch; Results/Review do not display preserved feedback settings or missing-legacy disclosure. [Drill.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/pages/Drill.tsx:550) still hardcodes `autoReveal = true`. Builder truthfully labels its LR set Training, but carrying that condition through saved attempt/reload/Continue/result interpretation is the real next gap.

2. **Review workflow lifecycle: implemented for a bounded set, broader source coverage genuinely incomplete.** [SavedReviewEpisodePanel.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/components/review/SavedReviewEpisodePanel.tsx:80) already saves optional review answer, distinct Reveal, notes, missed-only diagnoses, Mark reviewed, exact retry identity and teacher reason without changing original answer/flag. Tests cover these behaviors and actor/source switches. However [reviewEpisodes.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/lib/reviewEpisodes.ts:3) supports only `intro_lr_attempts` and `hs_capstone`. It is integrated into native-lab and Hard Sentences details. It is not a uniform durable episode workflow for LR journal, RC and generic Activity sources. Also it honestly says source feedback may already be visible, so recorded review answers do not reconstruct historical Blind Review. The correct remaining statement is **extend/validate the workflow across remaining source families**, not “build review episodes.”

3. **Library exercise resume/finish: implemented; chiefly remaining representative acceptance.** ActivityRunner already resumes the saved position/answers, distinguishes historical false completion, waits for account completion acknowledgement and retries retained closeout. Relevant tests: [ActivityRunner.resume.test.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityRunner.resume.test.tsx:125), [ActivityRunner.completion.test.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/features/activity-centre/components/ActivityRunner.completion.test.tsx:35). Native lab save already preserves immutable UUID/answer/ordinal and distinct retakes: [useNativeLabSave.test.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/bootcamps/main-conclusion/hooks/useNativeLabSave.test.ts:7). RRC acknowledged-closeout code is released. What is not established is the full ticket’s **two Activities plus one bootcamp actual saved resume/finish walk, including successful genuine RRC recording completion and teacher/student exact assignment parity**. Do not rebuild resume/finish.

4. **Exam per-question detail: already implemented; specific fields remain incomplete.** [PracticeTestRun.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/pages/PracticeTestRun.tsx:93) already has expandable section/question details and exact-question navigation, showing first answer, correct answer, time, flag and saved blind answer where available. Source identity is conveyed by test/section/question context. The current result model/UI does not expose question notes or durable review-task state. [practiceTestReview.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/lib/practiceTestReview.ts:70) explicitly sets RC `blindReviewAnswer: null`, so uniform RC blind-answer display is not implemented. Correct remaining item: **complete notes/review-state and unsupported blind-answer coverage, then accept the full report**, not “add an exam question table.”

5. **Study tools: implemented and published; remaining live acceptance rather than broad build work.** LR and RC already wire bookmark, cross-outs/elimination, highlights and existing find controls. [questionStudyTools.ts](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/lib/questionStudyTools.ts:1) separates these from attempts/flags/Review, isolates actor/family/question, preserves other-tab entries, and discloses browser-only storage. Integration tests cover selected-answer protection, bookmark controls, reload persistence, question-only clearing and actor changes: [StudyAids.integration.test.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/components/drill/official/StudyAids.integration.test.tsx:50), [RcPlayer.studyAids.test.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/features/rc/pages/RcPlayer.studyAids.test.tsx:94). Remaining narrow authenticated pane/responsive/keyboard/live checks should be stated as verification.

6. **Optional second attributed explanation: genuinely unimplemented in inspected production source.** Existing explanation components display bank/authored explanation material, but I found no second-explanation model with author/date and conditional rendering, and no corresponding feature tests. Search matches were ordinary educational prose, not a second-explanation feature. This is a distinct requirement; existing answer-choice reasoning and LSAT Lab explanation support do not satisfy attributed alternative explanations.

Current code basis is `abd65fc`, which parent’s independent fetch established matches `2fa3319` for source/docs; newer commit changes only question-bank JSON. Parent independently verified Lovable imported `2fa3319`, ready and published. No new tests or browser task walks were performed in this audit.

vi.hoisted(() => ({\\n  userId: \'11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111\',\\n  rows: new Map<string, unknown>(),\\n  failWrite: false,\\n}));\\n\\nvi.mock(\'@/integrations/supabase/client\', () => ({\\n  supabase: {\\n    auth: { getUser: async () => ({ data: { user: { id: account.userId } }, error: null }) },\\n    from: () => {\\n      const filters: Record<string, string> = {};\\n      const chain = {\\n        select: () => chain,\\n        eq: (field: string, value: string) => {\\n          filters[field] = value;\\n          return chain;\\n        },\\n        order: () => chain,\\n        range: async (start: number, end: number) => ({\\n          data: [...account.rows.entries()]\\n            .filter(([id]) => !filters.record_id || id === filters.record_id)\\n            .map(([record_id, data]) => ({ record_id, data }))\\n            .slice(start, end + 1),\\n          error: null,\\n        }),\\n        maybeSingle: async () => {\\n          const data = account.rows.get(filters.record_id);\\n          return { data: data ? { record_id: filters.record_id, data } : null, error: null };\\n        },\\n        upsert: async (row: { record_id: string; data: unknown }) => {\\n          if (account.failWrite) return { error: new Error(\'offline\') };\\n          account.rows.set(row.record_id, row.data);\\n          return { error: null };\\n        },\\n      };\\n      return chain;\\n    },\\n  },\\n}));\\n\\nimport {\\n  beginGuidedDrillCloseout,\\n  completeStandaloneDrillRun,\\n  createStandaloneDrillRun,\\n  listStandaloneDrillRuns,\\n  loadStandaloneDrillRun,\\n  saveStandaloneDrillRun,\\n} from \'./drillRunLedger\';\\n\\nconst snapshot = {\\n  questionQueue: [\'PT150-S1-Q1\', \'PT150-S1-Q2\'],\\n  currentIndex: 0,\\n  attempts: [{ qid: \'PT150-S1-Q1\', selected_answer: \'B\', correct: true, time_ms: 1200 }],\\n};\\n\\nbeforeEach(() => {\\n  account.userId = \'11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111\';\\n  account.rows.clear();\\n  account.failWrite = false;\\n});\\n\\ndescribe(\'standalone drill run ledger\', () => {\\n  it(\'keeps repeated assigned guided drill sittings separate, even after one is completed\', async () => {\\n    const key = \'guided::33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333\';\\n    const first = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, key, snapshot, { mode: \'type-drill\', label: \'Reasoning drill\' });\\n    await completeStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, first.id, snapshot);\\n    const second = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, key, {\\n      ...snapshot,\\n      attempts: [{ qid: \'PT150-S1-Q1\', selected_answer: \'C\', correct: false, time_ms: 2000 }],\\n    });\\n    expect(first.id).not.toBe(second.id);\\n    expect((await loadStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, first.id))?.session.status).toBe(\'completed\');\\n    expect((await loadStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, second.id))?.session.attempts[0].selected_answer).toBe(\'C\');\\n    expect((await listStandaloneDrillRuns(account.userId)).filter((row) => row.source_key === key)).toHaveLength(2);\\n  });\\n  it(\'freezes the first guided closeout answer across a failed final save and retry\', async () => {\\n    const key = \'guided::33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333\';\\n    const run = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, key, snapshot);\\n    const frozen = await beginGuidedDrillCloseout(account.userId, run.id, snapshot);\\n    expect(frozen.closeout_attempts?.[0].selected_answer).toBe(\'B\');\\n    const changed = { ...snapshot, attempts: [{ ...snapshot.attempts[0], selected_answer: \'C\', correct: false }] };\\n    account.failWrite = true;\\n    await expect(completeStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, run.id, changed)).rejects.toThrow(\'offline\');\\n    account.failWrite = false;\\n    const retried = await beginGuidedDrillCloseout(account.userId, run.id, changed);\\n    expect(retried.closeout_attempts?.[0].selected_answer).toBe(\'B\');\\n    const completed = await completeStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, run.id, changed);\\n    expect(completed.session.attempts[0].selected_answer).toBe(\'B\');\\n    expect(completed.session.status).toBe(\'completed\');\\n  });\\n  it(\'keeps two sittings of the same question pool with distinct IDs and results\', async () => {\\n    const key = \'drill::type-drill::abc\';\\n    const first = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, key, snapshot, { mode: \'type-drill\', label: \'Conclusion\' });\\n    const second = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, key, { ...snapshot, attempts: [] });\\n    expect(first.id).not.toBe(second.id);\\n    await completeStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, first.id, snapshot);\\n    const rows = await listStandaloneDrillRuns(account.userId);\\n    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);\\n    expect(rows.find((row) => row.id === first.id)?.session.status).toBe(\'completed\');\\n    expect(rows.find((row) => row.id === second.id)?.session.status).toBe(\'in_progress\');\\n  });\\n\\n  it(\'requires account acknowledgement and does not resurrect a completed run\', async () => {\\n    account.failWrite = true;\\n    await expect(createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, \'drill::adaptive\', snapshot)).rejects.toThrow(\'offline\');\\n    expect(account.rows.size).toBe(0);\\n    account.failWrite = false;\\n    const created = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, \'drill::adaptive\', snapshot);\\n    const completed = await completeStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, created.id, snapshot);\\n    const stale = await saveStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, created.id, { ...snapshot, attempts: [] });\\n    expect(stale).toEqual(completed);\\n    expect((await loadStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, created.id))?.session.status).toBe(\'completed\');\\n  });\\n\\n  it(\'rejects another account and ignores mismatched stored ownership\', async () => {\\n    const run = await createStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, \'drill::adaptive\', snapshot);\\n    account.userId = \'22222222-2222-4222-8222-222222222222\';\\n    await expect(loadStandaloneDrillRun(\'11111111-1111-4111-8111-111111111111\', run.id))\\n      .rejects.toThrow(\'Sign in\');\\n    expect(await loadStandaloneDrillRun(account.userId, run.id)).toBeNull();\\n    expect(await listStandaloneDrillRuns(account.userId)).toEqual([]);\\n  });\\n});\\n"}'}]

## assistant

Smallest safe implementation: **add optional, versioned LR run provenance to the existing JSON run/attempt records; preserve actual feedback behavior and disclose old rows. No schema migration or new Test mode needed.**

Critical correction: custom LR fixed sets use **manual Answer/Explanation**, not immediate feedback after Submit. [QuestionPicker.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/components/drill/QuestionPicker.tsx:145) says answers stay hidden unless Show Answer is chosen. [Drill.tsx](C:/Users/Joshu/dev/lsatu-review-197/src/pages/Drill.tsx:5186) hides Submit for fixed sets. Smart Drill is the actual immediate-after-submit path at line 5095. Derive provenance from `reviewPolicyForRun` and resolved launch kind, not a blanket assumption.

Suggested contract, optional so historical rows remain valid:

```ts
type LrRunProvenance = {
  version: 1;
  study_mode: 'training';
  feedback: 'manual_answer_and_explanation' | 'after_submit';
  launch_kind: /* existing supported resolved launch kinds */;
  source_key: string; // exact existing run identity
};
```

Use `manual_answer_and_explanation` for fixed standalone custom/saved-set launches; `after_submit` for adaptive Smart Drill. Keep unimplemented policies outside the enum.

Files and changes:

- **`src/lib/drillRunLedger.ts`**: add optional `provenance` to `StandaloneDrillRunSchema` and metadata. Validate consistency with source key and engine mode. Freeze it at creation, preserve from existing row on every save/finish, and never replace it from a changed launch URL. Stamp new persisted run attempts with the same source/feedback provenance.
- **`src/bootcamps/main-conclusion/persistence/records.ts`**: add optional provenance to `PracticeSessionAttempt` at line 184. Zod otherwise strips newly introduced attempt fields. Keep old attempts without metadata valid.
- **`src/types/drill.ts`**: add optional provenance to session and in-memory attempt types if the runner carries it through hydration/snapshot. Prefer session plus persisted attempts; avoid altering unrelated timed engine contracts.
- **`src/pages/Drill.tsx`**: derive provenance only for the supported LR standalone scope. Creation currently passes only mode/label at line 1175. Load acknowledged saved provenance during resume at line 974, before displaying/saving the restored session. Snapshot at line 1136 must retain per-attempt provenance. **Do not infer metadata for already answered legacy questions.** New answers in a resumed legacy run can record current actual behavior, while old answers remain unknown.
- **`src/pages/DrillRuns.tsx`**: display saved Training/feedback/source summary in both Results and Review, and visible “Feedback settings were not saved for this older sitting” when absent. Continue should retain exact run ID and saved policy. Existing Continue at line 56 launches generic questions plus `run`; saved row remains authority. Do not trust query provenance or change source identity. A supported manual fixed policy can continue through existing generic fixed-question launch; reject an incompatible saved policy rather than silently reinterpret.
- **Builder labels**: reuse their actual existing text; no new control needed.

Safest legacy policy: retain old run/answers verbatim, optional provenance absent, explicit disclosure. Avoid promoting current policy to historical fact merely because an old run is resumed. If metadata is per run only, retain its absence permanently for that old sitting and stamp policy only on new attempts; then display mixed/unknown coverage accurately.

Tests worth adding to existing suites:

1. `drillRunLedger.test.ts`: new Training/manual provenance survives create→save→load→finish; updater cannot replace frozen policy/source; old rows remain readable and unmodified; new versus old attempt metadata stays distinct.
2. `DrillRuns.test.tsx`: saved settings appear in Results and Review; old rows show disclosure; Continue retains exact run ID/source and does not invent Test behavior.
3. `Drill.reviewControls.test.tsx`: fixed-set picks/navigation still do not auto-reveal; resumed metadata matches policy; adaptive immediate feedback remains unchanged.
4. A runner hydration/snapshot regression proving settings and attempt metadata survive reload, with historical attempts staying unknown.

Do not overload `lr_attempts.mode` with Training labels. It currently records engine mode and feeds analytics. The bounded JSON contract satisfies saved sitting/attempt provenance for this slice; propagating it into the separate analytics table would require an explicitly reviewed schema/source-adapter change.

This is that name\\n * turned into the working shape the runner uses: the mode, the config, and the\\n * handful of ids that say which stored row this run belongs to.\\n *\\n * It replaces `drillRouteState`, which derived the same thing by re-reading the\\n * query string in a second, parallel grammar. There is now one reader — the\\n * codec in `drillLaunch` — and one shape, built here and handed down by\\n * `DrillLaunchGate`. The address bar keeps the short link it arrived with; the\\n * runner no longer needs it expanded into a long one.\\n *\\n * See `docs/adr/0016-a-drill-link-names-a-run-it-does-not-contain-one.md`.\\n */\\n\\nimport type {\\n  DrillMode,\\n  FullSectionConfig,\\n  TimerConfig,\\n  TypeDrillConfig,\\n} from \'@/types/drill\';\\nimport { DrillLaunchError, type DrillLaunch, type DrillLaunchBody, type PtLegRef } from \'@/lib/drillLaunch\';\\nimport type { DrillResumeIdentity } from \'@/lib/drillResumeSession\';\\n\\nexport interface DrillRunSpec {\\n  /**\\n   * The Drill Launch kind this run came from. `mode` is not the run kind (ADR\\n   * 0016: six kinds share `type-drill`), so anything that must tell a guided\\n   * drill from a filter drill or a Smart Drill reads this, never `mode`.\\n   */\\n  launchKind?: DrillLaunchBody[\'kind\'];\\n  mode?: DrillMode;\\n  config?: FullSectionConfig | TypeDrillConfig;\\n  /** Heading shown while the drill runs. */\\n  practicingLabel?: string;\\n  /** Where the runner\'s exit sends the student. Always app-relative. */\\n  returnTo?: string;\\n  /** True when the runner is launched from a Guided Drill. */\\n  isGuidedDrill?: boolean;\\n  guidedDurationMinutes?: number;\\n  /**\\n   * Presentation hint, independent of `mode`. When \'smart\', the runner renders\\n   * the adaptive Smart-Drill chrome even for a fixed-queue type-drill.\\n   */\\n  present?: \'smart\';\\n  /**\\n   * Seals the run: Answer and Explanation are not rendered at all, for the\\n   * whole session, for every question. A launch property and not a student\\n   * setting, because the person who assigns the section decides.\\n   */\\n  sealed?: boolean;\\n  /** Open on this question rather than the first. */\\n  initialIndex?: number;\\n  /**\\n   * A full-section run that must show only these questions: an assigned set\\n   * that happens to come from one section is not the whole section.\\n   */\\n  restrictQids?: string[];\\n\\n  // ── Which stored row this run belongs to ────────────────────────────────\\n  // Previously read straight off the query string by the runner, in a second\\n  // grammar that only agreed with the codec by coincidence.\\n  /** A classroom homework assignment. */\\n  assignmentId?: string;\\n  guidedSetId?: string;\\n  guidedDrillId?: string;\\n  guidedAssignmentId?: string;\\n  /** Intro to LR practice set: what it is, and which pool. */\\n  practiceSource?: string;\\n  practiceSlug?: string;\\n  /** Names the Run Record for a bootcamp day, so a re-draw cannot orphan it. */\\n  resumeIdentity?: DrillResumeIdentity;\\n  /** Which practice-test run and leg this section closes out. */\\n  ptRun?: PtLegRef;\\n}\\n\\nexport interface DrillTimerConfig {\\n  mode: \'countdown\' | \'stopwatch\';\\n  durationMs?: number;\\n}\\n\\nfunction typeDrillConfig(qids: string[]): TypeDrillConfig {\\n  return {\\n    qtypes: [],\\n    difficulties: [],\\n    pts: [],\\n    count: qids.length,\\n    selectedQids: qids,\\n  };\\n}\\n\\nconst QID_PATTERN = /^PT(\\\\d+)-S(\\\\d+)-Q\\\\d+$/;\\n\\n/**\\n * A homework set built from one real section — every qid comes from the same\\n * PT and section number, the shape `assign.ts` and the admin set builder both\\n * produce today. Such a set is a section wearing a homework row, not a mixed\\n * question list, so it runs under a real countdown instead of the untimed\\n * stopwatch every other homework set gets. A set that mixes sections (or PTs)\\n * returns null and keeps the untimed behaviour, because there is no one clock\\n * that honestly times a blend.\\n */\\nexport function singleSectionOf(qids: string[]): { pt: number; section: number } | null {\\n  if (qids.length === 0) return null;\\n  let pt: number | null = null;\\n  let section: number | null = null;\\n  for (const qid of qids) {\\n    const match = QID_PATTERN.exec(qid);\\n    if (!match) return null;\\n    const qPt = Number(match[1]);\\n    const qSection = Number(match[2]);\\n    if (pt === null) {\\n      pt = qPt;\\n      section = qSection;\\n    } else if (qPt !== pt || qSection !== section) {\\n      return null;\\n    }\\n  }\\n  return pt !== null && section !== null ? { pt, section } : null;\\n}\\n\\n/**\\n * Build the run spec from a launch.\\n *\\n * `resolvedQids` is how the id-named kinds arrive: their question list lives in\\n * a row, and `resolveDrillLaunch` reads that row before this is called.\\n */\\nexport function drillRunSpecFromLaunch(\\n  launch: DrillLaunch,\\n  resolvedQids?: string[],\\n  now: number = Date.now(),\\n): DrillRunSpec {\\n  const options = launch.options ?? {};\\n  const base: DrillRunSpec = {\\n    launchKind: launch.kind,\\n    returnTo: options.returnTo,\\n    practicingLabel: options.label,\\n    present: options.present,\\n    initialIndex: options.initialIndex,\\n  };\\n\\n  switch (launch.kind) {\\n    case \'smart-drill\':\\n      return { ...base, mode: \'adaptive\', sealed: false };\\n\\n    case \'section\': {\\n      const config: FullSectionConfig = {\\n        pt: launch.pt,\\n        section: launch.section,\\n        timer: {\\n          mode: launch.timerMode ?? (launch.minutes ? \'custom\' : \'35\'),\\n          customMinutes: launch.minutes,\\n          isPaused: false,\\n          elapsedMs: 0,\\n          startedAt: now,\\n        },\\n      };\\n      return {\\n        ...base,\\n        mode: \'full-section\',\\n        config,\\n        sealed: !!launch.sealed,\\n        ptRun: launch.ptLeg,\\n      };\\n    }\\n\\n    case \'questions\': {\\n      const carry = launch.carry;\\n      return {\\n        ...base,\\n        mode: carry?.mode ?? \'type-drill\',\\n        config: typeDrillConfig(launch.qids),\\n        sealed: carry?.sealed ?? false,\\n        isGuidedDrill: carry?.isGuidedDrill ?? false,\\n        guidedDurationMinutes: carry?.guidedDurationMinutes,\\n        assignmentId: carry?.assignmentId,\\n        guidedSetId: carry?.guidedSetId,\\n        guidedDrillId: carry?.guidedDrillId,\\n        guidedAssignmentId: carry?.guidedAssignmentId,\\n        practiceSource: carry?.practiceSource,\\n        practiceSlug: carry?.practiceSlug,\\n        resumeIdentity: carry?.resumeIdentity,\\n        ptRun: carry?.ptLeg,\\n      };\\n    }\\n\\n    case \'filter\':\\n      return {\\n        ...base,\\n        mode: \'type-drill\',\\n        config: {\\n          qtypes: launch.qtypes ?? [],\\n          difficulties: launch.difficulties ?? [],\\n          pts: launch.pts ?? [],\\n          sections: launch.sections,\\n          qfeatures: launch.qfeatures,\\n          count: launch.count,\\n        },\\n        sealed: false,\\n      };\\n\\n    case \'saved-set\':\\n    case \'assignment\':\\n    case \'guided-drill\':\\n    case \'practice-set\':\\n    case \'bootcamp-day\': {\\n      if (!resolvedQids) {\\n        throw new DrillLaunchError(\\n          `a ${launch.kind} launch must have its questions resolved before it can run`,\\n        );\\n      }\\n\\n      if (launch.kind === \'assignment\') {\\n        const section = singleSectionOf(resolvedQids);\\n        if (section) {\\n          return {\\n            ...base,\\n            mode: \'full-section\',\\n            config: {\\n              pt: section.pt,\\n              section: section.section,\\n              timer: {\\n                // An accommodated student carries her clock on the launch,\\n                // the same way a section sat from the section door does.\\n                mode: launch.timerMode ?? \'35\',\\n                isPaused: false,\\n                elapsedMs: 0,\\n                startedAt: now,\\n              },\\n            },\\n            // A section assigned as a measurement stays sealed until it is\\n            // scored. Blind review is where the thinking gets checked.\\n            sealed: !launch.revealAllowed,\\n            // A set drawn from one section is still only the questions that\\n            // were assigned. The runner shows these, never the whole section.\\n            restrictQids: resolvedQids,\\n            assignmentId: launch.assignmentId,\\n            guidedSetId: launch.guidedSetId,\\n          };\\n        }\\n      }\\n\\n      const spec: DrillRunSpec = {\\n        ...base,\\n        mode: \'type-drill\',\\n        config: typeDrillConfig(resolvedQids),\\n        sealed: false,\\n        isGuidedDrill: launch.kind === \'guided-drill\',\\n      };\\n      if (launch.kind === \'assignment\') {\\n        spec.assignmentId = launch.assignmentId;\\n        spec.guidedSetId = launch.guidedSetId;\\n      }\\n      if (launch.kind === \'guided-drill\') {\\n        spec.guidedDurationMinutes = launch.durationMinutes;\\n        spec.guidedDrillId = launch.drillId;\\n        spec.guidedAssignmentId = launch.assignmentId;\\n      }\\n      if (launch.kind === \'practice-set\') {\\n        // The two values the runner uses to recognise an Intro to LR practice\\n        // set and map it back to its module-progress completion id.\\n        spec.practiceSource = launch.source ?? \'intro-to-lr-practice-drills\';\\n        spec.practiceSlug = launch.slug;\\n      }\\n      if (launch.kind === \'bootcamp-day\') {\\n        // Keeps the Run Record named by the day rather than by the list the\\n        // picker happened to draw.\\n        spec.resumeIdentity = {\\n          kind: \'bootcamp-day\',\\n          day: launch.day,\\n          part: launch.part,\\n        };\\n      }\\n      return spec;\\n    }\\n  }\\n}\\n\\n/** The clock this run starts under. */\\nexport function timerConfigForRunSpec(\\n  spec: DrillRunSpec | null | undefined,\\n): DrillTimerConfig | null {\\n  if (spec?.isGuidedDrill && spec.guidedDurationMinutes) {\\n    return { mode: \'countdown\', durationMs: spec.guidedDurationMinutes * 60 * 1000 };\\n  }\\n\\n  if ((spec?.mode === \'type-drill\' || spec?.mode === \'practice-set\') && spec.config) {\\n    const config = spec.config as TypeDrillConfig;\\n    if (config.selectedQids && config.selectedQids.length > 0) {\\n      return { mode: \'stopwatch\' };\\n    }\\n  }\\n\\n  if (spec?.mode === \'full-section\' && spec.config) {\\n    const timer = (spec.config as FullSectionConfig).timer as TimerConfig;\\n\\n    if (timer.mode === \'unlimited\') {\\n      return { mode: \'stopwatch\' };\\n    }\\n\\n    let durationMs: number;\\n    switch (timer.mode) {\\n      case \'35\':\\n        durationMs = 35 * 60 * 1000;\\n        break;\\n      case \'52.5\':\\n        durationMs = 52.5 * 60 * 1000;\\n        break;\\n      case \'70\':\\n        durationMs = 70 * 60 * 1000;\\n        break;\\n      case \'custom\':\\n        durationMs = (timer.customMinutes || 35) * 60 * 1000;\\n        break;\\n      default:\\n        durationMs = 35 * 60 * 1000;\\n    }\\n\\n    return { mode: \'countdown\', durationMs };\\n  }\\n\\n  return { mode: \'stopwatch\' };\\n}\\n"}'}, {'type': 'input_text', 'text': '{"chunk_id":"c25a0a","wall_time_seconds":0.9813908,"exit_code":0,"original_token_count":0,"output":""}'}]

Runner/gate/pages remain parent-owned. No commit or push performed. Legacy singleton adoption must omit run metadata to avoid claiming historical feedback settings.

Expanding a unit reveals named lessons. A lesson opens a focused article page with title, bookmark, Ask, Notes, text, and optional video or transcript, plus a Done action. One reviewed lesson explicitly teaches what to examine after a missed question and directs students to Review Inbox. | **Adapt, P1:** Progress, resume, and a clear Done action can strengthen LSAT U\'s text-first Library. Instruction should point directly to the related practice/review behavior. No new video production is needed. |\\n153:| [Skills](https://lsatdemon.com/skills) and Skills History | Skills have small grouped units, completion states, question sequences, difficulty tags, and question-level explanations. A completed skill review displayed the question and an explanation panel with A–E rationales. Skills History lists started/completed instances with Continue. | **Adapt, P1:** Bounded reasoning tasks map well to LSAT U Activities and bootcamps. Keep the teacher\'s Bootcamps and Activities as separate Library tabs while letting each activity have a clear finish and review state. |\\n154:| [Resources](https://lsatdemon.com/resources) | Category links, article cards with author/date/summary, and pagination mix LSAT instruction, podcasts, and other topics. | **Selective, P2:** A searchable written resource index fits a text-first phase; avoid letting a large content feed displace assigned work or core study actions. |\\n155:| [Classes](https://lsatdemon.com/classes) | Chronological agenda shows time, title, level, subject, teacher, and registration state. A class detail shows duration, level, description, instructor, and a syllabus of exact linked questions. The visible Register button required a higher plan. | **Adapt, P1/P2:** Link Joshua\'s scheduled lesson to exact prep questions, then its recap and follow-up work. The prep links are the valuable feature even without a group-class catalog. |\\n156:| [Review Inbox](https://lsatdemon.com/reviews) | A separate queue has an Inbox Zero state, a Start Drilling action, and Recently done. A lesson says timed-section/test questions needing review are added here and can be marked done after understanding. This account\'s queue was empty. | **Adopt direction, P1:** LSAT U\'s approved Review page should have a short To review queue with a visible reason and Done state, then Wrong answers, Flagged, and teacher-selected filters. Empty-state copy should point to a sensible next action. |\\n157:| [Completed question review](https://lsatdemon.com/review/drill/52578486/test/141) | Focused three-column desktop layout: stimulus, question/choices, contextual explanation. The selected wrong letter is red; Reveal correct outlines the right choice green. The original answer remains visible. Explanations include a written argument analysis, choice-by-choice reasons, optional video, and links to a related lesson. A Review Checklist changes prompts between why the correct answer is right/predicted/rejected and why the chosen wrong answer was chosen/wrong/avoidable. Ask and Notes are adjacent tabs. | **Adopt/Adapt, P1:** This is the best verified model for LSAT U\'s journal/review page. Keep the question and first choice visible, show written reasons beside it, and prompt the student\'s own diagnosis before marking reviewed. Give Joshua a way to attach feedback to that exact step. The right explanation column was narrow at the observed laptop width, so a resizable or full-width reading state may be better. |\\n158:| [Advanced settings](https://lsatdemon.com/settings/advanced) and account menu | The profile menu groups Account, Advanced Settings, Test Settings, Plans, and Support. General has dark mode, high contrast, highlighter, and tip reset. Practice accommodations offer 35, 53, 70, or custom minutes and explicitly warn that this affects metrics. Review can show a checklist. Dashboard can hide Ratings. Test Settings opens an in-place panel with test pool status and locked tests. | **Adapt, P1/P2:** Tie accommodations to test timing and metrics; give students readable contrast and text highlighting. Use a small, consequence-explaining settings set rather than exposing every training control by default. |\\n"}'}]

## assistant

The largest useful cut is **stop expanding every teaching exercise into the full question-review system**. Keep current saved exercise results and their existing Review adapters; make ordinary LR/RC practice the dependable coaching loop first.

| Priority | Concrete cut or deferral | Purpose you give up | What to retain |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | Defer universal Review episodes across every Activity, bootcamp and historical exercise family (#166/#170/#175). | One uniform answer→blind answer→reveal→diagnosis→reviewed lifecycle for all content. This requires several source-specific identities, permissions and state contracts. | Existing exercise results, exact saved links, current native-lab/Hard Sentences episodes, and accurate coverage labels. Finish a bounded LR/RC loop rather than promising universal coverage. |
| **2** | Keep #178 deferred; stop treating complete Impeccable coverage as a prerequisite for functional closeout. | A comprehensive design critique and accessibility/performance review across seven destinations and all teacher rooms. | Fix observed material reading, navigation, keyboard, contrast and phone failures. Existing polish stays. |
| **3** | Defer adding every requested field/filter to Review, History and exam detail (#170/#172/#173). | Universal sortable tables with mode, blind answer, notes, review state, assignment link, reasoning, feature, difficulty and every cross-source filter. | Existing tables/filters and report details. Prioritize exact source, first answer, result, time and return link; add extra fields only when Joshua actually uses them. |
| **4** | Defer optional second explanations (#179). | Two distinctly authored reasoning paths, each with author/date. It adds content work as well as UI/data support. | Current written explanation and choice rationales. This is genuinely additional scope, unlike already shipped bookmarks/find/highlighting. |
| **5** | Stop expanding the Library topic pilot into broad authored hubs (#175). | A strategy/example/evidence/drill page for additional topics. | Existing Main Conclusion hub, current lessons, Activities and bootcamps. Two resumed/finished exercises plus one bootcamp remain a sensible representative completion check. |
| **6** | Defer extra study settings beyond timing, readable text and supported repeat behavior (#180/#179). | Additional prediction/training switches, feedback cadences, review rules and analytics-inclusion choices, each with persisted consequences. | Existing settings and the just-authorized truthful LR feedback provenance. Do not add Test mode merely to match competitors. |
| **7** | Defer the Schedule prep→recap→follow-up integration if sessions are already adequately handled through My Work and notes (#176). | Finding all three artifacts from one session. | Existing booking, timezone, Schedule and typed recaps. The implementation is already local, so this mainly avoids backend deployment and acceptance work; it is a smaller saving than cancelling a new feature. |
| **8** | Narrow exhaustive acceptance sweeps to representative flows and changed surfaces. | Certifying every source × role × state × width combination before closing the release. | One ordinary student and teacher loop, key direct-route checks, changed UI at phone/laptop/side-pane, saved resume/finish, and tests for known failure paths. Avoid repeatedly rechecking unchanged shipped surfaces. |

**Do not cut ownership, teacher-answer isolation, truthful save/completion, exact attempt identity, or first-answer denominator correctness.** These underpin trust rather than competitor resemblance. Missing source coverage can be explicitly excluded; incorrect records must not be silently included.

For timed work, separating **server protection** from **physical-device certification** is a worthwhile scope decision. Keep stale-writer/access-revocation enforcement and a real two-browser-context takeover/retry check. A physical laptop-plus-phone repetition can be deferred if the same backend/browser mechanism is already proven, with the limitation recorded. Do not replace server enforcement with only client pause tests. Likewise, stop designing an ever-growing network-failure matrix, but retain a meaningful lost-acknowledgement/idempotent retry check where duplicate or overwritten answers remain possible.

The competitive audit itself supports restraint: advanced error-pattern analytics depend on a reliable taxonomy; topic hubs, Study Quality behavior scores, import/merge, large resource indexes and extra training controls are P2/later patterns, not necessities for one-to-one teaching. Import, marketplaces, group classes, leaderboards, score prediction, capacity auto-planning and ownerless legacy import are already out of scope. Cancelling them again saves no additional work.

I cannot support reliable marginal-hour estimates from the plan. The best qualitative ordering is: **universal source/lifecycle expansion and exhaustive acceptance matrix save the most; broad polish and dense optional fields next; second explanations and topic expansion then; already-local Schedule saves less.** Existing published tables, study aids, drill lifecycle, weekly settings, protected timed runners and exercise save machinery should remain in place. No cancellation or ticket change was made.
